2026-10-10T01:11:52.7600206Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:aspire2ap] [nodes:2] Delegate max_context_len through the hybrid attention backend for XQA verify

The runtime-only XQA run 526019a (Actions 37945511358, PBS218219) got past
ServerArgs, XQA JIT and CUDA-graph capture, then failed on the first served
decode step in eagle_worker_common.build_eagle_verify_input:
"AttributeError: 'HybridAttnBackend' object has no attribute
'max_context_len'". Splitting prefill (fa3) and decode (trtllm_mha) wraps
the target backends in HybridAttnBackend, which delegates verify_mask to
its TARGET_VERIFY child but not max_context_len. That attribute exists
only on concrete backends.

Extend patches/sglang/decode-xqa-verify-mask.patch with a
max_context_len property on HybridAttnBackend returning the TARGET_VERIFY
child's value, matching the existing verify_mask delegation. A scan of the
fixed source for attn_backend attribute reads in the speculative, executor,
disaggregation and manager paths found no other attribute missing from the
base-class defaults. The build manifest lists the second target file.
Serving conditions are unchanged from 526019a (P3/D1 c72, page size 64,
decode verify on XQA, draft on FA3).

Validation: full CPU pytest including the actual five-patch chain on the
fixed source (asserting both edits), Ruff, bash -n.
